```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等（Redis `m17:idem:dept:config:{key}` TTL 24h）2) 调 M01-01 `depts/{code}` 校验科室存在且未停用（`"1704001"`）3) 校验类型与标志位：行政/后勤不得为收入中心（`"1704002"`）、内部服务方必须医技类（`"1704002"`）4) 校验参数区间：绩效系数 0.5~2.0、结余率目标 −1~1、基数为非负（`"1704045"`）5) 校验同科室同生效日不重复（`"1704046"`）6) 写入 `m17_dept_config` 7) 写日志 `CREATE_CONFIG` 8) 失效科室配置缓存。

**错误码：** `"1704001"`、`"1704002"`、`"1704045"`、`"1704046"`

#### API-05 修改科室核算配置

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m17/depts/configs/101
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "allocate_base_area": 380.00,
  "perf_coefficient": 1.100000,
  "target_surplus_amount": 550000.00,
  "remark": "科室扩面后调整面积与目标"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {"id": 101, "dept_name": "屈光科", "version": 2},
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验配置存在（`40400`）2) 若修改 `is_revenue_center`/`is_internal_provider`，重新校验类型约束（`"1704002"`）3) 校验参数区间（`"1704045"`）4) 乐观锁 `version` 冲突 `40900` 5) 写日志 `UPDATE_CONFIG`，`changed_fields` 记录变更前后值 6) 失效缓存 7) 若已存在当期损益，提示需重算（不阻断）。

**错误码：** `40400`、`"1704002"`、`"1704045"`、`40900`

#### API-06 同步科室主数据

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m17/depts/configs/sync
Content-Type: application/json

{"sync_mode": "INCREMENTAL", "overwrite_existing": false}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "added_count": 2,
    "updated_count": 1,
    "disabled_count": 0,
    "skipped_count": 9,
    "added_depts": [
      {"dept_id": 301, "dept_code": "DEPT301", "dept_name": "干眼科",
       "dept_type": "CLINICAL"}
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 调 M01-01 `depts/tree` 拉取全量科室树 2) `INCREMENTAL` 模式：仅新增本地缺失的科室、更新名称/层级变化、停用 M01 已停用的科室 3) `FULL` 模式：全量覆盖（需 `overwrite_existing=true`）4) 新增科室默认：`is_accounting_unit=true`、`is_cost_center=true`、`is_revenue_center` 按类型推断、`is_internal_provider=false` 5) 写日志 `CREATE_CONFIG`/`UPDATE_CONFIG` 6) 失效科室树与配置缓存 7) 返回统计。

**错误码：** `"1704001"`（M01 不可用）、`"179001"`

#### API-08 执行科室收入归集

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m17/depts/revenues/collect
Idempotency-Key: 5b4c3d2e-1f0a-9b8c-7d6e-5f4a3b2c1d0e
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "period": "2026-10",
  "biz_lines": ["OUTPATIENT", "INPATIENT", "REFRACTIVE"],
  "dept_ids": null,
  "rebuild": false,
  "collect_note": "2026 年 10 月科室收入归集"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "task_rev_20261031_0001",
    "batch_no": "REV202610-20261031-0001",
    "status": "PROCESSING",
    "estimated_seconds": 8,
    "period": "2026-10"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等 2) 校验期间状态 `OPEN`（`"1704003"`），非 `CLOSED`/`LOCKED`（`"1704025"`）3) 校验 M17-02 已完成该期间收入归集（`"1704005"`）4) 若已存在本期归集且未 `rebuild` → `"1704010"`；若 `rebuild` 则先把既有 `COLLECTED` 记录置 `VOIDED`（`CONFIRMED` 记录需先手工作废）5) 创建 Celery 任务 `m17.dept.revenue.collect`：按 `dept_id + biz_line + subject_code` 从 M17-02 取数分组；缺失科室维度时按 `m17_dept_config` 权重推算并标记 `is_estimated`，最后一个科室吸收尾差 6) 每行校验 `net = revenue − refund`（`"1704020"`）、退费 ≤ 收入（`"1704009"`）7) 返回任务 ID，前端轮询 8) 任务完成后写日志 `COLLECT_REVENUE` 并失效缓存。

**错误码：** `"1704003"`、`"1704005"`、`"1704007"`、`"1704009"`、`"1704010"`、`"1704020"`、`"1704025"`

```
{"confirm_note": "已核对推算记录，确认归集结果"}
```

**响应**

```json
    "status": "CONFIRMED"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按批次号查 `COLLECTED` 记录 2) 校验存在且未确认（`"1704023"`）3) 校验净收入勾稽（`"1704020"`）4) 批量置 `CONFIRMED` 并写 `collect_by` 5) 写日志 `CONFIRM_REVENUE` 6) 若该期间所有归集已确认，发事件 `m17.dept.revenue-confirmed`。

**错误码：** `"1704020"`、`"1704023"`、`"1704025"`

#### API-14 新增内部转移记录

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m17/depts/transfers
Idempotency-Key: 7d6e5f4a-3b2c-1d0e-9f8a-7b6c5d4e3f2a
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "period": "2026-10",
  "out_dept_id": 310,
  "in_dept_id": 205,
  "service_type": "LAB",
  "item_code": "LAB001",
  "quantity": 4500.00,
  "internal_price": 8.8000,
  "pricing_method": "COST_PLUS",
  "cost_ref_amount": 8.0000,
  "markup_rate": 0.100000,
  "source_ref_no": "M07-WL-202610-310",
  "remark": "屈光科术前检验内部结算"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 8801,
    "transfer_no": "TR202610-0001",
    "transfer_amount": "39600.00",
    "out_revenue_amount": "39600.00",
    "in_cost_amount": "39600.00",
    "status": "DRAFT",
    "balance_check_passed": true
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等 2) 校验期间可写（`"1704003"`/`"1704025"`）3) 校验科室存在（`"1704001"`）4) **转出科室必须是 `MEDICAL_TECH` 且 `is_internal_provider=TRUE`**（`"1704033"`）5) 转出 ≠ 转入（`"1704032"`）6) 校验服务量 > 0、单价 ≥ 0（`"1704031"`）7) `COST_PLUS` 必须填参考成本与加成率（`"1704034"`）；`GOVERNED_PRICE` 校验单价 ≤ 核定价（`"1704035"`）8) 计算 `transfer_amount = round(quantity × internal_price, 2)`，双向赋值 `out_revenue_amount = in_cost_amount = transfer_amount`（`"1704037"`）9) 生成 `transfer_no`（Redis `INCR m17:code:TR:{yyyyMM}`）10) 校验幂等键不重复（`"1704036"`）11) 写 `m17_dept_transfer`（`status='DRAFT'`）12) 写日志 `CREATE_TRANSFER`。

**错误码：** `"1704001"`、`"1704003"`、`"1704025"`、`"1704031"`、`"1704032"`、`"1704033"`、`"1704034"`、`"1704035"`、`"1704036"`、`"1704037"`

```
Content-Type: application/json

{

  "pricing_method": "COST_PLUS",

  "auto_confirm": false

```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",

    "imported_count": 0,
```

**错误码：** `"1704003"`、`"1704025"`、`"1704034"`、`"1704040"`

```json
       "is_tail_absorber": false},
      {"dept_id": 207, "dept_name": "视光中心", "allocate_base": "300.00",
       "allocate_ratio": "0.213828", "formula_amount": "10691.40",
       "allocated_amount": "10691.40", "tail_diff_amount": "0.00",
       "is_tail_absorber": false},
      {"dept_id": 208, "dept_name": "门诊部", "allocate_base": "153.00",
       "allocate_ratio": "0.109052", "formula_amount": "5452.60",
       "allocated_amount": "5452.60", "tail_diff_amount": "0.00",
       "is_tail_absorber": false},
      {"dept_id": 209, "dept_name": "眼底科", "allocate_base": "200.00",
       "allocate_ratio": "0.142552", "formula_amount": "7127.60",
       "allocated_amount": "7127.55", "tail_diff_amount": "-0.05",
       "is_tail_absorber": true}
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等 2) 校验期间可写（`"1704003"`/`"1704025"`）3) 校验 `pool_amount > 0`（`"1704011"`）4) 取参与科室（`include_dept_ids` 或全部 `is_cost_center=TRUE` 且 `is_accounting_unit=TRUE`，排除 `exclude_dept_ids`），科室数须在 1~200（`"1704012"`）5) 按 `allocate_method` 取分摊基数（面积/人数/工作量/收入/成本/固定比例）6) 计算 `base_total`，为 0 则 `"1704011"` 7) 按 §3.4.2 计算比例（6 位）、公式值（2 位）、实际分摊额（前 N−1 项 = 公式值，最后一项吸收尾差），科室按 `dept_id` 升序确定最后一项 8) 校验 `Σ allocated = pool_amount`（`"1704015"`）与尾差吸收项唯一（`"1704016"`）9) 校验同期间同池未分摊（`"1704014"`）10) 批量写 `m17_dept_allocation`（`status='CALCULATED'`）11) 写日志 `RUN_ALLOCATION` 12) 失效损益缓存 13) 返回完整明细供页面核对。

**错误码：** `"1704003"`、`"1704011"`、`"1704012"`、`"1704014"`、`"1704015"`、`"1704016"`、`"1704025"`

#### API-19 执行科室损益计算

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m17/depts/profits/calculate
Idempotency-Key: 0a1b2c3d-4e5f-6a7b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "period": "2026-10",
  "dept_ids": null,
  "data_status": "FINAL",
  "auto_allocate": true,
  "rebuild": false,
  "calc_note": "2026 年 10 月科室损益计算"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "batch_no": "PRF202610-20261031-0001",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "dept_count": 12,
    "total_revenue_amount": "9000500.00",
    "total_direct_cost_amount": "7560500.00",
    "total_allocated_cost_amount": "1440000.00",
    "total_surplus_amount": "1440000.00",
    "data_status": "FINAL",
    "checks": [
      {"item": "SURPLUS_FORMULA", "passed": true, "diff": "0.00"},
      {"item": "TOTAL_COST", "passed": true, "diff": "0.00"},
      {"item": "REVENUE_TOTAL", "passed": true, "diff": "0.00"},
      {"item": "TRANSFER_BALANCE", "passed": true, "diff": "0.00"},
      {"item": "ALLOCATION_BALANCE", "passed": true, "diff": "0.00"},
      {"item": "SUMMARY_DETAIL", "passed": true, "diff": "0.00"}
    ],
    "all_checks_passed": true,
    "profit_nos": ["DP202610-0001", "DP202610-0002"]
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等 2) 校验期间状态 `OPEN`（`"1704003"`），非 `CLOSED`/`LOCKED`（`"1704025"`）3) 校验收入已归集（`"1704005"`）、成本已归集（`"1704006"`）4) `auto_allocate=true` 时自动执行遗漏的费用池分摊；否则若有未分摊池 → `"1704013"` 5) 校验 `data_status`：调 M16-09 查日结是否封账，未封账而请求 `FINAL` → 强制降级为 `PROVISIONAL` 并在响应提示 6) 按 `dept_id` 逐科室：`revenue = Σ m17_dept_revenue.net_revenue_amount`；`direct_cost = Σ m17_dept_cost.cost_amount`；`allocated = Σ m17_dept_allocation.allocated_amount`；按 §3.4.1 计算 `total_cost`、`surplus`、`surplus_rate`、`cost_rate`（6 位小数，收入为 0 时比率置 0）7) 校验结余率 ∈ [−1, 1]（`"1704026"`）8) UPSERT `m17_dept_profit`（`uk_m17_dept_profit_key`；已存在且未 `rebuild` → `"1704030"`；`CONFIRMED` 且超 72 小时需审批 `"1704018"`）9) 生成明细 `m17_dept_profit_detail` 并校验 Σ明细 = 汇总（`"1704027"`）10) 执行六项勾稽校验（§3.4.7/§3.4.8），任一不通过 → `"1704021"` 并回滚 11) 写日志 `CALC_PROFIT` 12) 发事件 `m17.dept.profit-ready` 13) 失效缓存。

**错误码：** `"1704003"`、`"1704005"`、`"1704006"`、`"1704013"`、`"1704018"`、`"1704021"`、`"1704025"`、`"1704026"`、`"1704027"`、`"1704030"`、`"1704039"`

**错误码：** `"1704013"`、`"1704021"`、`"1704023"`、`"1704025"`

**错误码：** `"1704023"`、`"1704024"`

**错误码：** `"1704001"`、`"1704021"`、`"1704029"`

#### API-28 执行科室对比分析

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m17/depts/compares/run
Idempotency-Key: 1b2c3d4e-5f6a-7b8c-9d0e-1f2a3b4c5d6e
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "compare_type": "MOM",
  "period": "2026-10",
  "base_period": "2026-09",
  "compare_scope": "ALL",
  "dept_ids": null
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "batch_no": "CMP202610-20261031-0001",
    "compare_type": "MOM",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "base_period": "2026-09",
    "compare_scope": "ALL",
    "dept_count": 12,
    "rank_total": 12,
    "items": [
      {
        "compare_no": "CP202610-0002",
        "dept_id": 205,
        "dept_name": "屈光科",
        "cur_surplus_amount": "550000.00",
        "base_surplus_amount": "420000.00",
        "surplus_delta_amount": "130000.00",
        "surplus_growth_rate": "0.309524",
        "revenue_growth_rate": "0.141975",
        "surplus_rate_delta": "0.038038",
        "rank_in_scope": 3,
        "rank_percentile": "0.833333"
      }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等 2) 校验对比类型与基期（§5.8）：`RANK` 不得指定基期（`"1704042"`），其他必须指定且 ≠ 本期（`"1704041"`）3) 校验本期与基期均有损益数据（`"1704043"`）4) 按 `compare_scope` 确定对比范围科室集合，为空则 `"1704044"` 5) 按 §3.4.6 计算：增减额、增长率（**亏损科室用 `abs(基期结余)` 作分母**）、结余率变动、排名（结余降序）、百分位 `1 − (rank−1)/total`、目标达成率 6) UPSERT `m17_dept_compare`（`uk_m17_dept_compare_key`）7) 写日志 `RUN_COMPARE` 8) 返回全部对比结果。

**错误码：** `"1704041"`、`"1704042"`、`"1704043"`、`"1704044"`

#### API-29 科室对比分析结果列表

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m17/depts/compares?compare_type=MOM&period=2026-10
    &base_period=2026-09&compare_scope=ALL&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "compare_no": "CP202610-0002",
        "compare_type": "MOM",
        "period": "2026-10",
        "base_period": "2026-09",
        "dept_id": 205,
        "dept_name": "屈光科",
        "cur_revenue_amount": "1850000.00",
        "cur_surplus_amount": "550000.00",
        "cur_surplus_rate": "0.297297",
        "base_surplus_amount": "420000.00",
        "surplus_delta_amount": "130000.00",
        "surplus_growth_rate": "0.309524",
        "revenue_growth_rate": "0.141975",
        "rank_in_scope": 3,
        "rank_total": 12,
        "rank_percentile": "0.833333",
        "achievement_rate": "1.100000",
        "is_negative": false
      }
    ],
    "total": 12,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 1,
    "negative_depts": [
      {"dept_id": 290, "dept_name": "行政科", "cur_surplus_amount": "-480000.00",
       "base_surplus_amount": "-460000.00", "loss_change_amount": "20000.00"}
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按对比参数查 `m17_dept_compare`（`status<>'VOIDED'`）2) 排序 `rank_in_scope ASC`（`RANK` 类型）或 `surplus_growth_rate DESC` 3) `negative_depts` 列出本期亏损科室及亏损变化（`loss_change_amount = cur − base`，正数表示亏损扩大）4) 返回分页。

**错误码：** `"1704023"`

#### API-30 科室核算数据质量校验

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m17/depts/checks?period=2026-10
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "all_passed": true,
    "checks": [
      {

        "passed": true,
        "diff": "0.00",
        "failed_items": []
      },
      {
        "item": "TOTAL_COST",
        "label": "成本合计：Σ(直接+分摊) = Σ成本合计",
        "passed": true,
        "diff": "0.00",
        "failed_items": []
      },
      {
        "item": "REVENUE_TOTAL",
        "label": "收入合计：Σ(外部+内部) = Σ收入",
        "passed": true,
        "diff": "0.00",
        "failed_items": []
      },
      {
        "item": "TRANSFER_BALANCE",
        "label": "内部转移抵消：Σ转出收入 = Σ转入成本",
        "passed": true,
        "diff": "0.00",
        "failed_items": []
      },
      {
        "item": "ALLOCATION_BALANCE",
        "label": "分摊平衡：各池 Σ分摊额 = 池金额",
        "passed": true,

      },
      {
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 执行六项校验（§3.4.7/§3.4.8）：结余公式、成本合计、收入合计、内部转移抵消、分摊平衡、汇总明细一致 2) 每项返回差额与失败明细 3) `all_passed` 为全部通过标志 4) 数据质量统计 5) 该接口是 P05 确认按钮与定时巡检的共用校验入口。

**错误码：** `"1704003"`

#### API-31 导出科室损益 Excel

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m17/depts/export?period=2026-10&dept_type=CLINICAL&export_type=PROFIT
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "exp_20261031_m17_04_0001",
    "export_type": "PROFIT",
    "status": "PENDING",
    "estimated_seconds": 10,
    "max_rows": 50000
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m17:dept:export` 权限 2) `export_type ∈ {PROFIT, REVENUE, COST, TRANSFER, ALLOCATION, COMPARE, ALL}` 3) 创建 Celery 任务 `m17.dept.export`，生成多页签 Excel（汇总/明细/对比）4) 单次上限 50,000 行 5) 写日志 + 调 M02-06 审计（含金额数据）。

**错误码：** `40001`（超行数）、`"179001"`

#### API-32 科室损益查询（P-07 内部契约）★

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m17/dept/profit
    ?period=2026-10&dept_type=CLINICAL&order_by=SURPLUS_DESC&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "items": [
      {
        "dept_id": 205,
        "dept_code": "DEPT205",
        "dept_name": "屈光科",
        "dept_type": "CLINICAL",
        "dept_level": 1,
        "revenue_amount": "1850000.00",
        "direct_cost_amount": "980000.00",
        "allocated_cost_amount": "320000.00",
        "total_cost_amount": "1300000.00",
        "surplus_amount": "550000.00",
        "surplus_rate": "0.297297",
        "cost_rate": "0.702703",
        "target_achievement_rate": "1.100000",
        "data_status": "FINAL"
      }
    ],
    "total": 12,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 1,
    "summary": {
      "dept_count": 12,
      "total_revenue_amount": "9000500.00",
      "total_cost_amount": "7560500.00",
      "total_surplus_amount": "1440000.00",
      "avg_surplus_rate": "0.160000",
      "negative_dept_count": 4
    },
    "period_closed": false
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `period` 格式（`"1704039"`）2) 按 `period` + 可选筛选查 `m17_dept_profit`（`status<>'VOIDED'`），**走 `idx_m17_dept_profit_period (period, surplus_amount DESC)` 索引有序扫描** 3) 一次聚合生成 `summary`（避免 M22 二次聚合）4) `period_closed` 由 M17-01 期间状态判定，M22 据此决定是否标注「暂估」5) 结果缓存 Redis 120 秒（`m17:dept:profit:{period}:{hash}`），损益确认/发布/作废时主动失效 6) **只读，不产生任何写入** 7) SLA P95 ≤ 300ms。

**调用方：** M22 报表中心（科室损益报表、院领导驾驶舱、亏损预警）。

**M22 使用约定：**

| M22 场景 | 调用参数 |
|---|---|
| 科室损益报表 | `period` + 分页 |
| 驾驶舱「科室结余排行」 | `period` + `order_by=SURPLUS_DESC` + `page_size=10` |
| 亏损科室预警 | `period` + `only_negative=true` |
| 临床科室汇总 | `period` + `dept_type=CLINICAL` |
| 判断数据是否终稿 | 读 `period_closed` 与每项 `data_status` |

**错误码：** `"1704003"`、`"1704039"`、`"50300"`

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m17/dept.py
"""M17-04 科室核算与损益 —— 路由层"""

from typing import Literal, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, Query
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.api.deps import get_current_user, get_db, require_perm
from app.core.response import ApiResponse
from app.schemas.m17.dept import (
    AllocationRunIn,
    CompareRunIn,
    CostCollectIn,
    DeptConfigCreate,
    DeptConfigUpdate,
    DeptProfitListOut,
    DeptQueryIn,
    ProfitCalcIn,
    RevenueCollectIn,
    TransferCreate,
)
from app.services.m17.dept_service import DeptService
from app.services.m17.dept_allocation_service import DeptAllocationService
from app.services.m17.dept_profit_service import DeptProfitService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m17/depts", tags=["M17-04 科室核算与损益"])


def get_dept_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> DeptService:
    """构造科室核算服务。"""
    return DeptService(db)


def get_allocation_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> DeptAllocationService:
    """构造分摊服务。"""
    return DeptAllocationService(db)


def get_profit_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> DeptProfitService:
    """构造损益服务。"""
    return DeptProfitService(db)


# ---------- 工作台与统计 ----------

@router.get(
    "/dashboard",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="科室核算工作台",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:view"))],
)
async def get_dashboard(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7, description="会计期间 YYYY-MM"),
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """返回四步核算进度、关键指标、待处理事项、科室排行与趋势。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_dashboard(period, org_id))


@router.get(
    "/statistics",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="科室核算统计",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:report"))],
)
async def get_statistics(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    dept_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """按科室类型汇总、Top/Bottom 科室、数据质量。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_statistics(period, dept_type))


# ---------- 科室配置 ----------

@router.get(
    "/configs",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="科室核算配置列表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:view"))],
)
async def list_configs(
    dept_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    is_accounting_unit: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    effective_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按生效日过滤 yyyy-MM-dd"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """查询科室核算配置，支持按类型、核算单元标识、生效日筛选。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.list_configs(
        dept_type, is_accounting_unit, effective_date, page, page_size))


@router.post(
    "/configs",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="新增科室核算配置",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:admin"))],
)
async def create_config(
    body: DeptConfigCreate,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """校验科室存在、类型与标志位一致、参数区间后写入配置。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(
        await svc.create_config(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key))


# ---------- 收入 / 成本归集 ----------

@router.get(
    "/revenues",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="科室收入归集结果列表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:view"))],
)
async def list_revenues(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    biz_line: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    is_estimated: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """返回收入归集明细与汇总，并校验净收入 = 收入 − 退费。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.list_revenues(
        period, dept_id, biz_line, is_estimated, page, page_size))


@router.post(
    "/revenues/collect",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="执行科室收入归集",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:create"))],
)
async def collect_revenue(
    body: RevenueCollectIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """按业务线与科室拆分 M17-02 收入；缺科室维度时按权重推算并标记。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(
        await svc.collect_revenue(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key))


@router.post(
    "/costs/collect",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="执行科室成本归集",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:create"))],
)
async def collect_cost(
    body: CostCollectIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """按成本类型与科室拆分 M17-03 成本，并校核金额 = 数量 × 单价。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(
        await svc.collect_cost(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key))


# ---------- 内部转移价格 ----------

@router.get(
    "/transfers",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="内部转移记录列表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:view"))],
)
async def list_transfers(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    out_dept_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    in_dept_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    service_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """返回转移记录与双向平衡看板（转出收入 vs 转入成本）。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.list_transfers(
        period, out_dept_id, in_dept_id, service_type, page, page_size))


@router.post(
    "/transfers",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="新增内部转移记录",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:create"))],
)
async def create_transfer(
    body: TransferCreate,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptService = Depends(get_dept_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """转出科室须为医技内部服务方；转移金额双向记账。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(
        await svc.create_transfer(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key))


# ---------- 分摊 ----------

@router.post(
    "/allocations/run",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="执行间接费用分摊",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:create"))],
)
async def run_allocation(
    body: AllocationRunIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptAllocationService = Depends(get_allocation_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """按规则分摊费用池，最后一项吸收尾差，保证合计等于池金额。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(
        await svc.run(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key))


@router.get(
    "/allocations",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="分摊结果列表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:view"))],
)
async def list_allocations(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    pool_code: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
    svc: DeptAllocationService = Depends(get_allocation_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """返回分摊明细与按池的平衡校验结果。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.list_allocations(period, pool_code, page, page_size))


# ---------- 损益计算与状态流转 ----------

@router.post(
    "/profits/calculate",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="执行科室损益计算",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:create"))],
)
async def calculate_profit(
    body: ProfitCalcIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """计算结余 = 收入 − 直接成本 − 分摊成本，并执行六项勾稽校验。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(
        await svc.calculate(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key))


@router.post(
    "/profits/{period}/confirm",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="确认科室损益",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:audit"))],
)
async def confirm_profit(
    period: str,
    body: dict,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """勾稽全通过后批量确认，确认后金额锁定。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.confirm(period, body, operator=user))


@router.post(
    "/profits/{period}/publish",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="发布科室损益到 M22",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:report"))],
)
async def publish_profit(
    period: str,
    body: dict,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """仅财务主管可发布；暂估数据不可发布。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.publish(period, body, operator=user))


@router.post(
    "/profits/{period}/void",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="作废科室损益",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:audit"))],
)
async def void_profit(
    period: str,
    body: dict,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """作废本期损益并软删明细，需填作废原因。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.void(period, body, operator=user))


# ---------- 查询与下钻 ----------

@router.get(
    "/profits",
    response_model=ApiResponse[DeptProfitListOut],
    summary="科室损益分页列表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:view"))],
)
async def list_profits(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    dept_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    only_negative: bool = Query(False),
    order_by: Literal["SURPLUS_DESC", "SURPLUS_ASC", "REVENUE_DESC", "DEPT_CODE"] = Query(
        "SURPLUS_DESC"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[DeptProfitListOut]:
    """按结余/收入排序查询科室损益，附带本期汇总。"""
    q = DeptQueryIn(
        period=period, dept_type=dept_type, only_negative=only_negative,
        order_by=order_by, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.list_profits(q))


@router.get(
    "/profits/{profit_no}",

)

    profit_no: str,
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:

@router.get(
    "/profits/{profit_no}/details",

)

    profit_no: str,

    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:

@router.get(
    "/profits/{dept_id}/flow",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="科室损益四步穿透",

)
async def get_profit_flow(
    dept_id: int,
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """展示收入→直接成本→分摊→结余四步形成过程与勾稽结果。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_flow(dept_id, period))


# ---------- 对比分析 ----------

@router.post(
    "/compares/run",

    summary="执行科室对比分析",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:create"))],
)
async def run_compare(
    body: CompareRunIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """环比/同比/预算/排名对比，亏损科室增长率用绝对值作分母。"""

        await svc.run_compare(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key))


# ---------- 校验与导出 ----------

@router.get(
    "/checks",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="科室核算数据质量校验",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m17:dept:view"))],
)
async def run_checks(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """执行六项勾稽校验并返回逐项结果与差额。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.run_checks(period))


# ---------- 内部契约（M22 调用） ----------

internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m17", tags=["M17-04 内部契约"])


@internal_router.get(
    "/dept/profit",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="P-07 科室损益查询（供 M22）",
)
async def internal_dept_profit(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7, description="会计期间"),
    dept_type: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="科室类型"),
    only_negative: bool = Query(False, description="仅亏损科室"),
    order_by: str = Query("SURPLUS_DESC", description="排序方式"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    svc: DeptProfitService = Depends(get_profit_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """P-07：按期间返回科室损益列表与汇总，供 M22 报表消费。

    走 idx_m17_dept_profit_period 索引有序扫描，P95 ≤ 300ms。
    """
    q = DeptQueryIn(
        period=period, dept_type=dept_type, only_negative=only_negative,
        order_by=order_by, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.query_for_internal(q))
```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m17/dept_profit_service.py
"""M17-04 科室损益计算服务（核心）"""

from datetime import datetime
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m17.account_client import AccountPeriodClient
from app.clients.m17.m16_daily_client import M16DailyClient
from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m17.dept import (
    M17DeptAllocation,
    M17DeptCost,
    M17DeptProfit,
    M17DeptProfitDetail,
    M17DeptRevenue,
    M17DeptTransfer,
)
from app.schemas.m17.dept import DeptQueryIn, ProfitCalcIn
from app.services.m17.dept_log_service import DeptLogService

# 红线与阈值（与附录 E 系统参数一致）
RATE_MIN = Decimal("-1.000000")
RATE_MAX = Decimal("1.000000")
COST_RATE_MAX = Decimal("2.000000")
REBUILD_LIMIT_HOURS = 72
MONEY_Q = Decimal("0.01")
RATE_Q = Decimal("0.000001")


class DeptProfitService:
    """科室损益计算、勾稽校验与查询服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.log = DeptLogService(db)
        self.account = AccountPeriodClient()
        self.m16 = M16DailyClient()

    # ---------- §3.4.1 结余计算 ----------

    @staticmethod
    def compute_surplus(
        revenue: Decimal,
        direct_cost: Decimal,
        allocated_cost: Decimal,
    ) -> Dict[str, Decimal]:
        """计算科室结余、成本合计、结余率、成本率。

        公式（共享规范 §6.4）：
            surplus  = revenue − direct_cost − allocated_cost
            cost     = direct_cost + allocated_cost
            rate     = surplus / revenue      （revenue = 0 时置 0）
            cost_rate = cost / revenue        （revenue = 0 时置 0）
        """
        revenue = Decimal(str(revenue)).quantize(MONEY_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        direct_cost = Decimal(str(direct_cost)).quantize(MONEY_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        allocated = Decimal(str(allocated_cost)).quantize(MONEY_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

        total_cost = (direct_cost + allocated).quantize(MONEY_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        surplus = (revenue - direct_cost - allocated).quantize(
            MONEY_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

        if revenue == Decimal("0.00"):
            surplus_rate = Decimal("0.000000")
            cost_rate = Decimal("0.000000")
        else:
            # ★ 用未舍入的精确值比较，先算比率再舍入到 6 位
            surplus_rate = (surplus / revenue).quantize(
                RATE_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
            cost_rate = (total_cost / revenue).quantize(
                RATE_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

        return {
            "total_cost_amount": total_cost,
            "surplus_amount": surplus,
            "surplus_rate": surplus_rate,
            "cost_rate": cost_rate,
        }

    # ---------- 主计算入口 ----------

    async def calculate(
        self,
        req: ProfitCalcIn,
        operator: Any,
        idem_key: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行科室损益计算，写入核心表并执行六项勾稽校验。"""
        if idem_key:
            hit = await redis_client.get(f"m17:idem:dept:profit:{idem_key}")
            if hit:
                import json
                return json.loads(hit)

        period = req.period

        # 1) 期间状态
        period_status = await self.account.get_period_status(period)
        if period_status not in ("OPEN", "REOPENED"):
            raise BizException("1704003", f"会计期间 {period} 尚未开启或已结账")
        if period_status in ("CLOSED", "LOCKED"):
            raise BizException("1704025", f"会计期间 {period} 已结账，不可再修改")

        # 2) 前置：收入与成本已归集
        rev_cnt = await self._count(M17DeptRevenue, period)
        if rev_cnt == 0:
            raise BizException("1704005", "本期收入尚未归集，无法计算损益")
        cost_cnt = await self._count(M17DeptCost, period)
        if cost_cnt == 0:
            raise BizException("1704006", "本期成本尚未归集")

        # 3) 前置：分摊完成
        unallocated = await self._unallocated_pools(period)
        if unallocated and not req.auto_allocate:
            raise BizException(
                "1704013",
                f"还有 {len(unallocated)} 个费用池未分摊，结余将不完整",
                data={"unallocated_pools": unallocated},
            )

        # 4) 数据状态：上游未封账时强制降级为暂估
        if req.data_status == "FINAL" and not await self.m16.is_frozen(period):
            data_status = "PROVISIONAL"
        else:
            data_status = req.data_status

        # 5) 逐科室计算
        dept_ids = await self._accounting_dept_ids(period, req.dept_ids)
        profits: List[M17DeptProfit] = []
        batch_no = f"PRF{period.replace('-', '')}-{datetime.now():%Y%m%d}-0001"

        for dept_id in dept_ids:
            revenue = await self._sum_revenue(period, dept_id)
            direct = await self._sum_direct_cost(period, dept_id)
            allocated = await self._sum_allocation(period, dept_id)

            calc = self.compute_surplus(revenue, direct, allocated)

            # 结余率区间校验（§3.4.1 边界条件）
            if not (RATE_MIN <= calc["surplus_rate"] <= RATE_MAX):
                raise BizException(
                    "1704026",
                    f"科室 {dept_id} 结余率 {calc['surplus_rate']} 超出合理区间",
                )
            if calc["cost_rate"] > COST_RATE_MAX:
                raise BizException(
                    "1704026",
                    f"科室 {dept_id} 成本率 {calc['cost_rate']} 超出合理区间",
                )

            existing = await self._get_profit(period, dept_id)
            if existing is not None:
                if existing.status == "CONFIRMED":
                    age_hours = (datetime.now() - existing.confirm_at).total_seconds() / 3600
                    if age_hours > REBUILD_LIMIT_HOURS and not req.rebuild:
                        raise BizException(
                            "1704018",
                            f"科室 {dept_id} 已确认超过 {REBUILD_LIMIT_HOURS} 小时，"
                            f"重建需财务主管审批",
                        )
                if not req.rebuild:
                    raise BizException(
                        "1704030",
                        f"科室 {dept_id} 在期间 {period} 已存在损益记录",
                    )
                existing.status = "VOIDED"

            profit = await self._upsert_profit(
                period, dept_id, calc, data_status, batch_no, operator)
            profits.append(profit)
            await self._build_details(profit)

        await self.db.flush()

        # 6) 六项勾稽校验
        checks = await self.run_checks(period, raise_on_fail=False)
        if not checks["all_passed"]:
            await self.db.rollback()
            failed = [c for c in checks["checks"] if not c["passed"]]
            raise BizException(
                "1704021",
                f"勾稽校验未通过：{failed[0]['label']} 差额 {failed[0]['diff']} 元",
                data={"checks": checks["checks"]},
            )

        await self.log.record(
            period=period,
            entity_type="PROFIT",
            action="CALC_PROFIT",
            action_desc=f"科室损益计算完成：{len(profits)} 个科室，"
                        f"结余合计 {checks['summary']['total_surplus_amount']} 元",
            batch_no=batch_no,
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()
        await self._invalidate_cache(period)

        result = {
            "batch_no": batch_no,
            "period": period,
            "dept_count": len(profits),
            "total_revenue_amount": checks["summary"]["total_revenue_amount"],
            "total_direct_cost_amount": checks["summary"]["total_direct_cost_amount"],
            "total_allocated_cost_amount": checks["summary"]["total_allocated_cost_amount"],
            "total_surplus_amount": checks["summary"]["total_surplus_amount"],
            "data_status": data_status,
            "checks": checks["checks"],
            "all_checks_passed": True,
            "profit_nos": [p.profit_no for p in profits],
        }
        if idem_key:
            import json
            await redis_client.setex(
                f"m17:idem:dept:profit:{idem_key}", 86400,
                json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
        return result

    # ---------- §3.4.7 / §3.4.8 六项勾稽校验 ----------

    async def run_checks(self, period: str,
                         raise_on_fail: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行六项勾稽校验，返回逐项结果。"""
        checks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        # 1) 结余公式：Σ(revenue − direct − allocated) = Σsurplus
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(
                    M17DeptProfit.revenue_amount
                    - M17DeptProfit.direct_cost_amount
                    - M17DeptProfit.allocated_cost_amount), 0),
            ).where(
                M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).one()
        checks.append(self._check("SURPLUS_FORMULA",
                                  "结余公式：Σ(收入−直接−分摊) = Σ结余",
                                  Decimal(str(row[1])), Decimal(str(row[0]))))

        # 2) 成本合计：Σ(direct + allocated) = Σtotal_cost
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.total_cost_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(
                    M17DeptProfit.direct_cost_amount
                    + M17DeptProfit.allocated_cost_amount), 0),
            ).where(
                M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).one()
        checks.append(self._check("TOTAL_COST",
                                  "成本合计：Σ(直接+分摊) = Σ成本合计",
                                  Decimal(str(row[1])), Decimal(str(row[0]))))

        # 3) 收入合计：Σ(external + internal) = Σrevenue
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.revenue_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(
                    M17DeptProfit.revenue_external_amount
                    + M17DeptProfit.revenue_internal_amount), 0),
            ).where(
                M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).one()
        checks.append(self._check("REVENUE_TOTAL",
                                  "收入合计：Σ(外部+内部) = Σ收入",
                                  Decimal(str(row[1])), Decimal(str(row[0]))))

        # 4) 内部转移抵消：Σ out_revenue = Σ in_cost
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptTransfer.out_revenue_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptTransfer.in_cost_amount), 0),
            ).where(
                M17DeptTransfer.period == period,
                M17DeptTransfer.status.in_(["CONFIRMED", "POSTED"]),
                M17DeptTransfer.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).one()
        checks.append(self._check("TRANSFER_BALANCE",
                                  "内部转移抵消：Σ转出收入 = Σ转入成本",
                                  Decimal(str(row[0])), Decimal(str(row[1]))))

        # 5) 分摊平衡：各池 Σ allocated = pool_amount 且尾差项唯一
        pool_rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(
                M17DeptAllocation.pool_code,
                func.max(M17DeptAllocation.pool_amount),
                func.sum(M17DeptAllocation.allocated_amount),
                func.count().filter(M17DeptAllocation.is_tail_absorber.is_(True)),
            ).where(
                M17DeptAllocation.period == period,
                M17DeptAllocation.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptAllocation.is_deleted.is_(False),
            ).group_by(M17DeptAllocation.pool_code)
        )).all()
        alloc_diff = Decimal("0.00")
        failed_pools: List[str] = []
        for pool_code, pool_amount, allocated_total, tail_cnt in pool_rows:
            diff = (Decimal(str(pool_amount))
                    - Decimal(str(allocated_total))).quantize(MONEY_Q)
            if diff != Decimal("0.00") or int(tail_cnt) != 1:
                alloc_diff += abs(diff)
                failed_pools.append(pool_code)
        checks.append({
            "item": "ALLOCATION_BALANCE",
            "label": "分摊平衡：各池 Σ分摊额 = 池金额",
            "passed": not failed_pools,
            "diff": str(alloc_diff),
            "failed_items": failed_pools,
        })

        # 6) 汇总明细一致
        detail_rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(
                M17DeptProfitDetail.line_type,
                func.sum(M17DeptProfitDetail.amount),
            ).where(
                M17DeptProfitDetail.period == period,
                M17DeptProfitDetail.is_deleted.is_(False),
            ).group_by(M17DeptProfitDetail.line_type)
        )).all()
        detail_map = {r[0]: Decimal(str(r[1])) for r in detail_rows}
        sum_revenue = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.revenue_amount), 0)).where(
                M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar()
        sum_direct = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.direct_cost_amount), 0)).where(
                M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar()
        sum_alloc = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.coalesce(
                func.sum(M17DeptProfit.allocated_cost_amount), 0)).where(
                M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar()

        rev_detail = (detail_map.get("REVENUE", Decimal("0.00"))
                      + detail_map.get("REVENUE_REFUND", Decimal("0.00")))
        diff6 = (
            abs(rev_detail - Decimal(str(sum_revenue)))
            + abs(detail_map.get("DIRECT_COST", Decimal("0.00"))
                  - Decimal(str(sum_direct)))
            + abs(detail_map.get("ALLOCATED_COST", Decimal("0.00"))
                  - Decimal(str(sum_alloc)))
        ).quantize(MONEY_Q)
        checks.append({
            "item": "SUMMARY_DETAIL",
            "label": "汇总明细一致：Σ明细 = 汇总",
            "passed": diff6 == Decimal("0.00"),
            "diff": str(diff6),
            "failed_items": [],
        })

        summary = self._build_summary(period)
        all_passed = all(c["passed"] for c in checks)
        if not all_passed and raise_on_fail:
            failed = [c for c in checks if not c["passed"]]
            raise BizException(
                "1704021",
                f"勾稽校验未通过：{failed[0]['label']} 差额 {failed[0]['diff']} 元",
                data={"checks": checks},
            )
        return {
            "period": period,
            "all_passed": all_passed,
            "checks": checks,
            "summary": summary,
            "data_quality": await self._data_quality(period),
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _check(item: str, label: str, expected: Any, actual: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """构造单条校验结果，严格相等（无容差）。"""
        exp = Decimal(str(expected)).quantize(MONEY_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        act = Decimal(str(actual)).quantize(MONEY_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        diff = (exp - act).quantize(MONEY_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        return {
            "item": item,
            "label": label,
            "passed": diff == Decimal("0.00"),
            "diff": str(diff),
            "failed_items": [],
        }

    # ---------- P-07 契约实现 ----------

    async def query_for_internal(self, q: DeptQueryIn) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-07：供 M22 报表查询科室损益。

        走 idx_m17_dept_profit_period (period, surplus_amount DESC) 有序扫描，
        附带 summary 避免 M22 二次聚合，Redis 缓存 120 秒。
        """
        cache_key = f"m17:dept:profit:{q.period}:{q.model_dump_json()}"
        cached = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
        if cached:
            import json
            return json.loads(cached)

        result = await self._query_profits(q)

        import json
        await redis_client.setex(
            cache_key, 120, json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
        return result

    async def _query_profits(self, q: DeptQueryIn) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """分页查询科室损益并附带汇总。"""
        conds = [
            M17DeptProfit.period == q.period,
            M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
            M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False),
        ]
        if q.dept_type:
            conds.append(M17DeptProfit.dept_type == q.dept_type)
        if q.dept_level:
            conds.append(M17DeptProfit.dept_level == q.dept_level)
        if q.only_negative:
            conds.append(M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount < 0)
        if q.min_surplus_amount is not None:
            conds.append(M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount >= q.min_surplus_amount)
        if q.max_surplus_amount is not None:
            conds.append(M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount <= q.max_surplus_amount)

        order_map = {
            "SURPLUS_DESC": M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount.desc(),
            "SURPLUS_ASC": M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount.asc(),
            "REVENUE_DESC": M17DeptProfit.revenue_amount.desc(),
            "DEPT_CODE": M17DeptProfit.dept_code.asc(),
        }

        total = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.count()).select_from(M17DeptProfit).where(*conds)
        )).scalar() or 0

        rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M17DeptProfit)
            .where(*conds)
            .order_by(order_map[q.order_by])
            .offset((q.page - 1) * q.page_size)
            .limit(q.page_size)
        )).scalars().all()

        summary_row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(
                func.count(),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.revenue_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.total_cost_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount), 0),
                func.count().filter(M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount < 0),
            ).where(*conds)
        )).one()

        total_revenue = Decimal(str(summary_row[1]))
        total_surplus = Decimal(str(summary_row[3]))
        avg_rate = (Decimal("0.000000") if total_revenue == Decimal("0")
                    else (total_surplus / total_revenue).quantize(
                        RATE_Q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP))

        period_status = await self.account.get_period_status(q.period)
        return {
            "period": q.period,
            "items": [self._to_out(r) for r in rows],
            "total": total,
            "page": q.page,
            "page_size": q.page_size,
            "pages": (total + q.page_size - 1) // q.page_size,
            "summary": {
                "dept_count": int(summary_row[0]),
                "total_revenue_amount": str(total_revenue),
                "total_cost_amount": str(Decimal(str(summary_row[2]))),
                "total_surplus_amount": str(total_surplus),
                "avg_surplus_rate": str(avg_rate),
                "negative_dept_count": int(summary_row[4]),
            },
            "period_closed": period_status in ("CLOSED", "LOCKED"),
        }

    # ---------- 私有工具 ----------

    async def _count(self, model: Any, period: str) -> int:
        """统计某表在期间内的有效记录数。"""
        return (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.count()).select_from(model).where(
                model.period == period,
                model.status != "VOIDED",
                model.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar() or 0

    async def _sum_revenue(self, period: str, dept_id: int) -> Decimal:
        """科室收入 = Σ 净收入。"""
        val = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptRevenue.net_revenue_amount), 0))
            .where(
                M17DeptRevenue.period == period,
                M17DeptRevenue.dept_id == dept_id,
                M17DeptRevenue.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptRevenue.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar()
        return Decimal(str(val))

    async def _sum_direct_cost(self, period: str, dept_id: int) -> Decimal:
        """科室直接成本 = Σ 成本金额。"""
        val = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptCost.cost_amount), 0))
            .where(
                M17DeptCost.period == period,
                M17DeptCost.dept_id == dept_id,
                M17DeptCost.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptCost.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar()
        return Decimal(str(val))

    async def _sum_allocation(self, period: str, dept_id: int) -> Decimal:
        """科室分摊成本 = Σ 实际分摊额。"""
        val = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptAllocation.allocated_amount), 0))
            .where(
                M17DeptAllocation.period == period,
                M17DeptAllocation.dept_id == dept_id,
                M17DeptAllocation.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptAllocation.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar()
        return Decimal(str(val))

    async def _get_profit(self, period: str, dept_id: int) -> Optional[M17DeptProfit]:
        """取既有损益记录。"""
        return (await self.db.execute(
            select(M17DeptProfit).where(
                M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                M17DeptProfit.dept_id == dept_id,
                M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()

    async def _build_summary(self, period: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """构造本期汇总。"""
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.revenue_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.direct_cost_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.allocated_cost_amount), 0),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(M17DeptProfit.surplus_amount), 0),
            ).where(
                M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).one()
        return {
            "total_revenue_amount": str(Decimal(str(row[0]))),
            "total_direct_cost_amount": str(Decimal(str(row[1]))),
            "total_allocated_cost_amount": str(Decimal(str(row[2]))),
            "total_surplus_amount": str(Decimal(str(row[3]))),
        }

    async def _invalidate_cache(self, period: str) -> None:
        """失效该期间损益缓存。"""
        pattern = f"m17:dept:profit:{period}:*"
        async for key in redis_client.scan_iter(match=pattern):
            await redis_client.delete(key)
```

**代码说明：**

| 类/方法 | 职责 | 关键点 |
|---|---|---|
| `compute_surplus` | §3.4.1 结余计算 | 公式 `surplus = revenue − direct − allocated`；收入为 0 时比率置 0；**分项求和后再算比率**，不取平均 |
| `calculate` | 损益计算主入口 | 期间/归集/分摊三重前置校验；上游未封账强制降级 `PROVISIONAL`；逐科室计算后**整体事务**，勾稽失败则 `rollback` |
| `run_checks` | §3.4.7/§3.4.8 六项勾稽 | 每项返回期望值/实际值/差额；**严格相等无容差**；分摊池按 `pool_code` 分组校验尾差项唯一 |
| `_check` | 单项校验构造 | `ROUND_HALF_UP` 到分后比较，差额为 0 才通过 |
| **`query_for_internal`** | **★ P-07 契约** | 走 `idx_m17_dept_profit_period` 有序扫描；附带 `summary` 避免二次聚合；Redis 120 秒缓存 |
| `_query_profits` | 分页 + 汇总 | 一次聚合取 summary，避免 N+1；`period_closed` 供 M22 判断是否标暂估 |
| `_sum_revenue` / `_sum_direct_cost` / `_sum_allocation` | 三路取数 | 均按 `dept_id + period` 且排除 `VOIDED`，走 `idx_*_period_dept` 索引 |

---

---

## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 | 容量估算 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m17:dept:dashboard:{period}:{org}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 工作台四步进度与排行 | 归集/分摊/损益任一环节完成后删 | 12 月 × 3KB ≈ 36KB |
| `m17:dept:profit:{period}:{query_hash}` | String(JSON) | 120s | **P-07 科室损益查询结果** | 损益确认/发布/作废/重算时按期间前缀删 | 12 月 × 5 查询 × 8KB ≈ 480KB |
| `m17:dept:config:{dept_id}` | String(JSON) | 1800s | 科室核算配置（分摊基数、目标） | 配置新增/修改/同步时删 | 120 × 1KB ≈ 120KB |
| `m17:dept:depttree` | String(JSON) | 1800s | 科室树（逐级汇总用） | M01-01 科室变更事件 | 1 × 50KB ≈ 50KB |
| `m17:dept:stats:{period}:{type}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 统计报表 | 到期自然失效 | 12 × 4KB ≈ 48KB |
| `m17:dept:checks:{period}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 六项勾稽校验结果 | 任一归集/分摊/损益变更后删 | 12 × 3KB ≈ 36KB |
| `m17:code:{prefix}:{yyyyMM}` | String(INT) | 48h | 单号计数器（DP/TR/AL/CP） | 每日 03:00 与 DB `max()` 对账 | 4 前缀 ≈ 1KB |
| `m17:idem:dept:{biz}:{key}` | String(JSON) | 24h | 幂等键（collect/allocate/calculate/compare） | 到期自然失效 | 200/日 × 4KB ≈ 800KB |
| `m17:lock:dept:period:{period}` | String | 300s | **期间级分布式锁（防并发归集/分摊/计算）** | 任务完成释放或超时 | 并发 3 × 100B ≈ 1KB |
| `m17:lock:dept:report:{period}` | String | 120s | 报表重算锁 | 完成释放 | 并发 5 × 100B ≈ 1KB |

**总容量估算：约 1.6MB**（Redis 7 单实例可承载）。

**缓存一致性策略：**

| 缓存 | 一致性要求 | 策略 |
|---|---|---|
| **`m17:dept:profit`（P-07）** | **强一致优先** | 损益状态变更（确认/发布/作废/重算）后按 `period` 前缀 `SCAN + DEL`；TTL 120s 兜底 |
| `checks` | 强一致优先 | 任一归集/分摊/损益写入后 `DEL` |
| `config` / `depttree` | 允许 30 分钟延迟 | M01-01 事件驱动失效 + TTL 兜底 |
| `dashboard` / `stats` | 允许 60~300s 延迟 | Cache-Aside |

```python
# app/core/cache/m17_dept_cache.py
"""M17-04 科室核算缓存封装"""

import hashlib
from typing import Any, Optional

from app.core.redis import redis_client


class DeptCache:
    """科室核算缓存管理。"""

    DASHBOARD_TTL = 60
    PROFIT_TTL = 120
    CONFIG_TTL = 1800
    STATS_TTL = 300
    CHECKS_TTL = 60

    @staticmethod
    def dashboard_key(period: str, org_id: Optional[int]) -> str:
        return f"m17:dept:dashboard:{period}:{org_id or 0}"

    @staticmethod
    def profit_key(period: str, query_repr: str) -> str:
        """P-07 缓存键：按查询参数哈希，避免超长 key。"""
        digest = hashlib.md5(query_repr.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
        return f"m17:dept:profit:{period}:{digest}"

    @staticmethod
    def config_key(dept_id: int) -> str:
        return f"m17:dept:config:{dept_id}"

    @staticmethod
    def checks_key(period: str) -> str:
        return f"m17:dept:checks:{period}"

    async def invalidate_period(self, period: str) -> None:
        """期间数据变更后统一失效：P-07、工作台、校验结果。"""
        for pattern in (
            f"m17:dept:profit:{period}:*",
            f"m17:dept:dashboard:{period}:*",
            f"m17:dept:stats:{period}:*",
        ):
            async for key in redis_client.scan_iter(match=pattern):
                await redis_client.delete(key)
        await redis_client.delete(self.checks_key(period))

    async def invalidate_config(self, dept_id: int) -> None:
        """配置变更后失效单科室配置与科室树。"""
        await redis_client.delete(self.config_key(dept_id),
                                  "m17:dept:depttree")


class DeptPeriodLock:
    """期间级分布式锁：防止并发归集/分摊/计算导致数据错乱。"""

    TTL_SECONDS = 300

    @staticmethod
    def key(period: str, biz: str) -> str:
        return f"m17:lock:dept:period:{biz}:{period}"

    @classmethod
    async def acquire(cls, period: str, biz: str, token: str) -> bool:
        """获取期间锁，成功返回 True。"""
        return bool(await redis_client.set(
            cls.key(period, biz), token, nx=True, ex=cls.TTL_SECONDS))

    @classmethod
    async def release(cls, period: str, biz: str, token: str) -> None:
        """释放期间锁（校验 token 防止误释放他人锁）。"""
        key = cls.key(period, biz)
        current = await redis_client.get(key)
        if current and current.decode() == token:
            await redis_client.delete(key)
```

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

| 任务名 | 触发时机 | 处理内容 | 重试策略 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `m17.dept.revenue.collect` | P02 执行归集 | 按科室拆分 M17-02 收入，推算并标记，尾差吸收 | 重试 2 次，指数退避 |
| `m17.dept.cost.collect` | P03 执行归集 | 按科室拆分 M17-03 成本，校核金额公式 | 重试 2 次 |
| `m17.dept.transfer.import` | P04 导入工作量 | 调 M07 取服务量，批量生成转移记录 | 重试 3 次 |
| `m17.dept.allocation.run` | P05 执行分摊 | 按规则分摊，尾差吸收，平衡校验 | 重试 2 次 |
| `m17.dept.profit.calculate` | P05 执行损益 | 逐科室计算并六项勾稽 | 不重试（需人工介入） |
| `m17.dept.compare.run` | P07 执行对比 | 环比/同比/排名计算 | 重试 2 次 |
| `m17.dept.export` | 用户触发 | 生成多页签 Excel | 重试 2 次 |
| `m17.dept.daily.patrol` | APScheduler 每日 | 扫描未归集/未分摊/未确认的期间并提醒 | 不重试 |
| `m17.dept.check.patrol` | APScheduler 每日 | 对已确认期间执行勾稽巡检，异常告警 | 不重试 |
| `m17.dept.code.reconcile` | APScheduler 每日 03:00 | 单号计数器与数据库对账 | 不重试 |
| `m17.dept.log.partition` | APScheduler 每月 20 日 | 预建下下月日志分区；DETACH 36 月前分区 | 重试 2 次 |

```python
# app/tasks/m17/dept_tasks.py
"""M17-04 科室核算 Celery 异步任务"""

import asyncio
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from celery import shared_task
from celery.utils.log import get_task_logger
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app.core.session import get_async_session
from app.models.m17.dept import (
    M17DeptAllocation,
    M17DeptProfit,
    M17DeptRevenue,
)
from app.services.m17.dept_log_service import DeptLogService

logger = get_task_logger(__name__)


@shared_task(name="m17.dept.profit.calculate", bind=True, max_retries=0)
def calculate_profit_task(self, period: str, batch_no: str,
                          data_status: str = "FINAL") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """执行科室损益计算（异步）。

    不重试：勾稽失败意味着数据有问题，自动重试只会重复失败，
    必须人工排查后重新触发。这是财务任务的通用原则。
    """
    from app.services.m17.dept_profit_service import DeptProfitService

    async def _run() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        async with get_async_session() as db:
            svc = DeptProfitService(db)
            from app.schemas.m17.dept import ProfitCalcIn
            req = ProfitCalcIn(period=period, data_status=data_status,
                               auto_allocate=True, rebuild=False)
            return await svc.calculate(req, operator=_system_operator())

    try:
        result = asyncio.run(_run())
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("科室损益计算失败 period=%s batch=%s: %s",
                     period, batch_no, exc)
        raise
    logger.info("科室损益计算完成 period=%s 科室数=%s 结余=%s",
                period, result.get("dept_count"),
                result.get("total_surplus_amount"))
    return result


@shared_task(name="m17.dept.check.patrol")
def patrol_period_checks() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """每日勾稽巡检：对已确认期间的科室损益重新校验。

    已确认的数据仍可能因上游补录而变化，必须每日巡检发现漂移。
    """
    async def _run() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        from app.services.m17.dept_profit_service import DeptProfitService

        alerts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        async with get_async_session() as db:
            periods = (await db.execute(
                select(M17DeptProfit.period).where(
                    M17DeptProfit.status.in_(["CONFIRMED", "PUBLISHED"]),
                    M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False),
                ).group_by(M17DeptProfit.period).order_by(
                    M17DeptProfit.period.desc()).limit(12)
            )).scalars().all()

            svc = DeptProfitService(db)
            for period in periods:
                try:
                    result = await svc.run_checks(period, raise_on_fail=False)
                except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                    logger.warning("期间 %s 勾稽巡检异常：%s", period, exc)
                    alerts.append({"period": period, "error": str(exc)})
                    continue
                if not result["all_passed"]:
                    failed = [c for c in result["checks"] if not c["passed"]]
                    alerts.append({
                        "period": period,
                        "failed_items": [c["item"] for c in failed],
                        "diffs": [c["diff"] for c in failed],
                    })
                    await DeptLogService(db).record_system(
                        action="CALC_PROFIT",
                        action_desc=f"期间 {period} 勾稽巡检未通过："
                                    f"{[c['item'] for c in failed]}",
                        entity_type="PROFIT",
                        period=period,
                    )
            await db.commit()
        return alerts

    alerts = asyncio.run(_run())
    if alerts:
        logger.error("勾稽巡检发现 %d 个期间异常：%s", len(alerts), alerts[:3])
    else:
        logger.info("勾稽巡检全部通过")
    return {"alert_count": len(alerts), "alerts": alerts}


@shared_task(name="m17.dept.daily.patrol")
def patrol_pending_periods() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """每日巡检：发现上一期间未完成的核算环节并提醒。"""
    async def _run() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        pending: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        async with get_async_session() as db:
            period = _previous_period()
            rev_cnt = (await db.execute(
                select(func.count()).select_from(M17DeptRevenue).where(
                    M17DeptRevenue.period == period,
                    M17DeptRevenue.status != "VOIDED",
                    M17DeptRevenue.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalar() or 0
            if rev_cnt == 0:
                pending.append({"period": period, "item": "REVENUE_NOT_COLLECTED"})

            alloc_cnt = (await db.execute(
                select(func.count()).select_from(M17DeptAllocation).where(
                    M17DeptAllocation.period == period,
                    M17DeptAllocation.status != "VOIDED",
                    M17DeptAllocation.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalar() or 0
            if alloc_cnt == 0:
                pending.append({"period": period, "item": "ALLOCATION_NOT_RUN"})

            profit_cnt = (await db.execute(
                select(func.count()).select_from(M17DeptProfit).where(
                    M17DeptProfit.period == period,
                    M17DeptProfit.status != "VOIDED",
                    M17DeptProfit.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalar() or 0
            if profit_cnt == 0:
                pending.append({"period": period, "item": "PROFIT_NOT_CALCULATED"})
        return pending

    pending = asyncio.run(_run())
    if pending:
        logger.warning("上一期间 %s 尚有未完成核算环节：%s", _previous_period(), pending)
    return {"period": _previous_period(), "pending": pending}


def _previous_period() -> str:
    """返回上一会计期间（YYYY-MM）。"""
    now = datetime.now()
    if now.month == 1:
        return f"{now.year - 1}-12"
    return f"{now.year}-{now.month - 1:02d}"


def _system_operator() -> Any:
    """构造系统操作人上下文（任务触发时无登录用户）。"""
    class _SystemOperator:
        id = 0
        name = "SYSTEM"
        role = "SYSTEM"
    return _SystemOperator()
```

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务 ID | Cron 表达式 | 时区 | 说明 | 失败处理 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m17_dept_period_remind` | `0 9 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 月初提醒上月核算未完成 | 记日志 |
| `m17_dept_check_patrol` | `0 2 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 每日勾稽巡检（近 12 个已确认期间） | 记日志 + 告警 |
| `m17_dept_code_reconcile` | `0 3 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 单号计数器对账 | 记日志 |
| `m17_dept_log_partition` | `0 2 20 * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 日志分区预建与归档 | 重试 2 次 |

```python
# app/jobs/m17/dept_jobs.py
"""M17-04 科室核算 APScheduler 定时任务注册"""

from apscheduler.schedulers.asyncio import AsyncIOScheduler
from apscheduler.triggers.cron import CronTrigger

from app.tasks.m17.dept_tasks import (
    patrol_pending_periods,
    patrol_period_checks,
)

TIMEZONE = "Asia/Shanghai"


def register_dept_jobs(scheduler: AsyncIOScheduler) -> None:
    """注册 M17-04 全部定时任务。"""
    scheduler.add_job(
        patrol_period_checks,
        CronTrigger(hour=2, minute=0, timezone=TIMEZONE),
        id="m17_dept_check_patrol",
        name="科室损益勾稽巡检",
        replace_existing=True,
        max_instances=1,
        coalesce=True,
        misfire_grace_time=3600,
    )
    scheduler.add_job(
        patrol_pending_periods,
        CronTrigger(hour=9, minute=0, timezone=TIMEZONE),
        id="m17_dept_period_remind",
        name="核算未完成提醒",
        replace_existing=True,
        max_instances=1,
        coalesce=True,
        misfire_grace_time=1800,
    )
```

---

## 8. 业务规则与校验

### 8.1 编码规则

| 编码对象 | 规则 | 生成方式 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 科室损益单 `DP` | `DP` + YYYYMM + `-` + 4 位流水 | Redis `INCR m17:code:DP:{yyyyMM}` | — |
| 内部转移单 `TR` | `TR` + YYYYMM + `-` + 4 位流水 | Redis `INCR m17:code:TR:{yyyyMM}` | — |
| 分摊单 `AL` | `AL` + YYYYMM + `-` + 4 位流水 | Redis `INCR m17:code:AL:{yyyyMM}` | — |
| 对比单 `CP` | `CP` + YYYYMM + `-` + 4 位流水 | Redis `INCR m17:code:CP:{yyyyMM}` | — |
| 归集批次 `REV`/`CST` | `REV`/`CST` + YYYYMM + `-` + YYYYMMDD + `-` + 4 位 | Redis `INCR` | — |
| 日志流水 `DL` | `DL` + yyyyMMddHHmmss + 6 位 | 应用生成 | — |

> **单号格式校验：** 数据库 CHECK 强制正则（如 `ck_m17_dept_profit_no_format: profit_no ~ '^DP[0-9]{6}-[0-9]{4}$'`）。
> **对账：** 每日 03:00 比较 Redis 计数器与数据库当日 `max()`，不一致以数据库为准重置并告警。

### 8.2 唯一性规则

| 对象 | 唯一约束 | 范围 | 违反时错误码 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 科室损益单号 | `uk_m17_dept_profit_no` | 全局 | `40900` | — |
| **科室损益（科室×期间）** | **`uk_m17_dept_profit_key`** | **`period, dept_id`** | **`"1704030"`** | **一个科室一个期间只有一条** |
| 科室配置（科室×生效日） | `uk_m17_dept_config_dept_eff` | `dept_id, effective_from` | `"1704046"` | 版本化 |
| 收入归集幂等键 | `uk_m17_dept_revenue_key` | `period, dept_id, biz_line, revenue_type, subject_code, item_code, source_ref_no` | `40900` | 防重复归集 |
| 成本归集幂等键 | `uk_m17_dept_cost_key` | `period, dept_id, cost_type, cost_nature, subject_code, item_code, source_ref_no` | `40900` | 同上 |
| 转移单号 | `uk_m17_dept_transfer_no` | 全局 | `40900` | — |
| 转移记录幂等键 | `uk_m17_dept_transfer_key` | `period, out_dept_id, in_dept_id, service_type, item_code, source_ref_no` | `"1704036"` | 防重复转移 |
| 分摊单号 | `uk_m17_dept_allocation_no` | 全局 | `40900` | — |
| **分摊（池×科室）** | **`uk_m17_dept_allocation_key`** | **`period, pool_code, dept_id`** | **`"1704014"`** | 一池对一科室只分摊一次 |
| **尾差吸收项** | **`uk_m17_dept_allocation_tail`** | **`period, pool_code`** WHERE `is_tail_absorber` | **`"1704016"`** | **每池仅一个尾差吸收项** |
| 对比单号 | `uk_m17_dept_compare_no` | 全局 | `40900` | — |
| 对比记录 | `uk_m17_dept_compare_key` | `compare_type, period, base_period, compare_scope, dept_id` | `40900` | 同参数覆盖更新 |
| 明细行 | `uk_m17_dept_profit_detail_key` | `profit_id, line_type, subject_code, item_code, pool_code` | `40900` | — |

### 8.3 状态流转规则

遵循 §3.2.3 的 6 条流转规则。实现层面的强约束：

| # | 规则 | 实现层面 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 状态只能经 `DeptProfitStateMachine.transition()` 变更 | Service 层唯一入口；Router 不直接写 `status` | `"1704023"` |
| 2 | 每次状态变更必须写 `m17_dept_log`（含 `from_status`/`to_status`） | 状态机内置日志调用 | — （内部保证） |
| 3 | 终态（`VOIDED`）不可再流转 | 状态机白名单 | `"1704023"` |
| 4 | `CONFIRMED` 后金额字段只读 | Service 层校验 + 需先作废 | `"1704023"` |
| 5 | `PROVISIONAL` 不可发布 | API-21 校验 `data_status` | `"1704022"` |
| 6 | 已结账期间不可归集/分摊/计算/作废 | 调 M17-01 查期间状态 | `"1704025"` |
| 7 | 发布仅财务主管 | 角色校验 | `"1704028"` |
| 8 | 已确认超 72 小时重建需审批 | 时间差校验 | `"1704018"` |

```python
# app/services/m17/dept_state_machine.py
"""M17-04 科室损益状态机（唯一入口）"""

from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Set, Tuple

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.models.m17.dept import M17DeptProfit
from app.services.m17.dept_log_service import DeptLogService

TRANSITIONS: Dict[Tuple[str, str], str] = {
    ("CALCULATED", "CONFIRM"): "CONFIRMED",
    ("CONFIRMED", "PUBLISH"): "PUBLISHED",
    ("PUBLISHED", "UNPUBLISH"): "CONFIRMED",
    ("CALCULATED", "VOID"): "VOIDED",
    ("CONFIRMED", "VOID"): "VOIDED",
    ("PUBLISHED", "VOID"): "VOIDED",
}

TERMINAL: Set[str] = {"VOIDED"}
EDITABLE: Set[str] = {"CALCULATED"}


class DeptProfitStateMachine:
    """科室损益状态机：唯一的状态变更入口。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.log = DeptLogService(db)

    async def transition(
        self,
        profit: M17DeptProfit,
        event: str,
        operator: Any,
        reason: Optional[str] = None,
        extra: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> str:
        """执行状态流转，校验合法性并写审计日志。"""
        if profit.status in TERMINAL:
            raise BizException(
                "1704023", f"状态已处于终态 {profit.status}，不可再流转")

        target = TRANSITIONS.get((profit.status, event))
        if target is None:
            raise BizException(
                "1704023",
                f"当前状态 {profit.status} 不允许执行动作 {event}",
            )

        from_status = profit.status
        profit.status = target
        for field, value in (extra or {}).items():
            if hasattr(profit, field):
                setattr(profit, field, value)

        await self.log.record(
            period=profit.period,
            entity_type="PROFIT",
            entity_id=profit.id,
            action=event,
            action_desc=reason or f"{from_status} → {target}",
            from_status=from_status,
            to_status=target,
            amount_before=profit.surplus_amount,
            amount_after=profit.surplus_amount,
            operator=operator,
        )
        return target

    @staticmethod
    def assert_editable(profit: M17DeptProfit) -> None:
        """校验损益可编辑（仅 CALCULATED）。"""
        if profit.status not in EDITABLE:
            raise BizException(
                "1704023",
                f"当前状态 {profit.status} 不允许修改，请先作废",
            )
```

### 8.4 业务校验规则

#### 8.4.1 科室配置校验

| # | 场景 | 校验规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 科室存在 | M01-01 查得且未停用 | `"1704001"` |
| 2 | 科室类型合法 | 6 类之一 | `40001` |
| 3 | 行政/后勤不得为收入中心 | `dept_type NOT IN ('ADMIN','LOGISTICS') OR is_revenue_center=FALSE` | `"1704002"` |
| 4 | 内部服务方必须医技 | `is_internal_provider → dept_type='MEDICAL_TECH'` | `"1704002"` |
| 5 | 绩效系数区间 | 0.5 ~ 2.0 | `"1704045"` |
| 6 | 结余率目标区间 | −1.0 ~ 1.0 | `"1704045"` |
| 7 | 分摊基数非负 | 面积/人数/工作量 ≥ 0 | `"1704045"` |
| 8 | 生效区间有序 | `effective_to IS NULL OR effective_to >= effective_from` | `"1704045"` |
| 9 | 同科室同生效日唯一 | `uk_m17_dept_config_dept_eff` | `"1704046"` |

#### 8.4.2 收入归集校验

| # | 场景 | 校验规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 期间可写 | `OPEN`/`REOPENED` | `"1704003"`/`"1704025"` |
| 2 | 上游已归集 | M17-02 有数据 | `"1704005"` |
| 3 | 未重复归集 | 无既有记录或已 `rebuild` | `"1704010"` |
| 4 | 推算权重有效 | 权重合计 > 0 | `"1704007"` |
| 5 | 科室数合理 | 1 ~ 200 | `"1704008"` |
| 6 | 退费不超收入 | `cost_refund_amount <= revenue_amount` | `"1704009"` |
| 7 | **净收入恒等** | `net = revenue − refund` | `"1704020"` |
| 8 | 收入科目存在 | M17-01 查得 | `"1704004"` |
| 9 | 推算须有规则 | `is_estimated → estimate_rule` ≥5 字 | `40001` |

#### 8.4.3 成本归集校验

| # | 场景 | 校验规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 期间可写 | 同上 | `"1704003"`/`"1704025"` |
| 2 | 上游已归集 | M17-03 有数据 | `"1704006"` |
| 3 | 未重复归集 | 同上 | `"1704010"` |
| 4 | **金额公式** | `abs(cost_amount − round(qty×price, 2)) <= 0.01` | `"1704017"` |
| 5 | 金额非负 | `cost_amount >= 0` | `"1704009"` |
| 6 | 分项不超合计 | `Σ分项 − 合计 <= 0.01` | `"1704019"` |
| 7 | 转移成本有来源 | `cost_nature='TRANSFER' → source_dept_id` | `40001` |

#### 8.4.4 内部转移校验

| # | 场景 | 校验规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **转出科室为医技内部服务方** | `dept_type='MEDICAL_TECH' AND is_internal_provider=TRUE` | **`"1704033"`** |
| 2 | 转出 ≠ 转入 | `out_dept_id <> in_dept_id` | `"1704032"` |
| 3 | 服务量 > 0 | `quantity > 0` | `"1704031"` |
| 4 | 单价 ≥ 0 | `internal_price >= 0` | `"1704031"` |
| 5 | 成本加成参数 | `COST_PLUS → cost_ref_amount AND markup_rate` | `"1704034"` |
| 6 | 核定价上限 | `GOVERNED_PRICE → internal_price <= 核定价` | `"1704035"` |
| 7 | 未重复转移 | `uk_m17_dept_transfer_key` | `"1704036"` |
| 8 | **金额公式** | `abs(transfer_amount − round(qty×price, 2)) <= 0.01` | `"1704037"` |
| 9 | **双向平衡** | `out_revenue_amount = in_cost_amount = transfer_amount` | **`"1704038"`** |
| 10 | 加成率区间 | −0.5 ~ 2.0 | `"1704045"` |

#### 8.4.5 分摊校验

| # | 场景 | 校验规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 待分摊总额 > 0 | `pool_amount > 0` | `"1704011"` |
| 2 | 基数合计 > 0 | `base_total > 0` | `"1704011"` |
| 3 | 科室数合理 | 1 ~ 200 | `"1704012"` |
| 4 | 未重复分摊 | `uk_m17_dept_allocation_key` | `"1704014"` |
| 5 | **合计等于池额** | `Σ allocated_amount = pool_amount` | **`"1704015"`** |
| 6 | **尾差项唯一** | `uk_m17_dept_allocation_tail` 命中 1 条 | **`"1704016"`** |
| 7 | 比例一致性 | `abs(ratio − round(base/base_total, 6)) <= 0.000001` | `"1704015"` |
| 8 | 尾差标记一致 | `is_tail_absorber ↔ tail_diff_amount ≠ 0` | `"1704016"` |
| 9 | 期间可写 | 同上 | `"1704003"`/`"1704025"` |

#### 8.4.6 损益计算校验

| # | 场景 | 校验规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 期间可写 | `OPEN`/`REOPENED` | `"1704003"`/`"1704025"` |
| 2 | 收入已归集 | 有归集记录 | `"1704005"` |
| 3 | 成本已归集 | 有归集记录 | `"1704006"` |
| 4 | 分摊已完成 | 无未分摊池 | `"1704013"` |
| 5 | **结余公式** | `surplus = revenue − direct − allocated` | **`"1704021"`** |
| 6 | 成本合计 | `total_cost = direct + allocated` | `"1704021"` |
| 7 | 收入合计 | `revenue = external + internal` | `"1704021"` |
| 8 | 结余率区间 | −1.0 ~ 1.0 | `"1704026"` |
| 9 | 成本率区间 | 0 ~ 2.0 | `"1704026"` |
| 10 | 汇总明细一致 | `Σ明细 = 汇总` | `"1704027"` |
| 11 | 未重复计算 | 无既有或已 `rebuild` | `"1704030"` |
| 12 | 重建时间窗 | `CONFIRMED` 超 72 小时需审批 | `"1704018"` |
| 13 | 内部转移抵消 | `Σout = Σin` | `"1704039"` |

#### 8.4.7 状态与发布校验

| # | 场景 | 校验规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 状态允许 | 状态机白名单 | `"1704023"` |
| 2 | 作废原因 | ≥10 字 | `"1704024"` |
| 3 | 确认需勾稽全通过 | 六项全 `passed` | `"1704021"` |
| 4 | 发布需终稿 | `data_status='FINAL'` | `"1704022"` |
| 5 | 发布需财务主管 | 角色校验 | `"1704028"` |
| 6 | 有损益数据 | 期间有记录 | `"1704029"` |
| 7 | 发布需已确认 | 全部 `CONFIRMED` | `"1704023"` |

#### 8.4.8 对比分析校验

| # | 场景 | 校验规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | RANK 无基期 | `compare_type='RANK' → base_period IS NULL` | `"1704042"` |
| 2 | 非 RANK 有基期 | 且 `base_period <> period` | `"1704041"` |
| 3 | 基期有数据 | 基期损益存在 | `"1704043"` |
| 4 | 范围非空 | 对比范围内科室数 > 0 | `"1704044"` |
| 5 | 亏损增长率分母 | 用 `abs(base_surplus)` | — （计算规则） |
| 6 | 排名范围 | `1 <= rank <= rank_total` | `"1704045"` |

### 8.5 计算规则

| 计算项 | 公式 | 精度/舍入 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **科室结余** | `revenue − direct_cost − allocated_cost` | 2 位小数 `ROUND_HALF_UP` | §3.4.1，DB CHECK 强制 |
| 成本合计 | `direct_cost + allocated_cost` | 2 位小数 | DB CHECK 强制 |
| 收入合计 | `external + internal` | 2 位小数 | DB CHECK 强制 |
| **结余率** | `surplus / revenue` | 6 位小数 `ROUND_HALF_UP` | 收入为 0 时置 0 |
| 成本率 | `total_cost / revenue` | 6 位小数 | 收入为 0 时置 0 |
| 平均结余率 | `Σsurplus / Σrevenue` | 6 位小数 | **分项求和后再算，不取平均** |
| 目标达成率 | `surplus / target_surplus` | 6 位小数 | 目标为 0 时不计算 |
| **分摊比例** | `allocate_base / base_total` | 6 位小数 | §3.4.2 |
| 分摊公式值 | `pool_amount × ratio` | 2 位小数 | — |
| **实际分摊额** | 前 N−1 项 = 公式值；最后一项 = 池额 − 前 N−1 之和 | 2 位小数 | **尾差吸收** |
| 尾差 | `allocated − formula` | 2 位小数 | 仅最后一项非 0 |
| 转移金额 | `quantity × internal_price` | 2 位小数 | 容差 0.01 |
| 净收入 | `revenue − refund` | 2 位小数 | DB CHECK 强制 |
| 成本金额 | `round(quantity × unit_price, 2)` | 2 位小数 | DB CHECK 校核 |
| 收入增减额 | `cur − base` | 2 位小数 | — |
| **结余增长率** | `(cur − base) / abs(base)` | 6 位小数 | **亏损用绝对值作分母** |
| 收入增长率 | `(cur − base) / base` | 6 位小数 | base=0 时为 NULL |
| 结余率变动 | `cur_rate − base_rate` | 6 位小数 | — |
| 排名百分位 | `1 − (rank − 1) / total` | 6 位小数 | — |
| 明细占比 | `amount / 同类合计` | 6 位小数 | 预计算，避免查询时算除法 |
| 推算金额 | `总额 × weight`，最后一项吸收尾差 | 2 位小数 | 推算时同分摊逻辑 |

> **统一舍入：** 所有中间计算用 `Decimal`（精度 28 位），仅在最终输出时 `ROUND_HALF_UP` 到规定精度。**校验比较一律使用舍入后的值严格相等（无容差）**，唯金额公式类校验允许 0.01 元容差（处理 4 位单价与 6 位比率的舍入）。

### 8.6 引用与删除规则

| 对象 | 删除方式 | 约束 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 科室配置 | 置 `status='INACTIVE'` | 有历史归集不可物理删 | `"1704023"` |
| 收入/成本归集 | 置 `VOIDED`（`rebuild` 时批量） | `CONFIRMED` 需先手工作废 | `"1704010"` |
| 内部转移 | 置 `VOIDED` | `POSTED` 需先冲销 | `"1704023"` |
| 分摊 | 置 `VOIDED` | 已参与损益需先作废损益 | `"1704023"` |
| 科室损益 | 置 `VOIDED` + 明细软删 | 期间已结账不可作废 | `"1704025"` |
| 损益明细 | 随损益一起软删 | 不可单独删除 | — |
| 对比分析 | 置 `VOIDED` | — | — |
| 日志 | 按月分区归档，36 个月后 DETACH | 不可删单条 | — |

**引用完整性（应用层校验，无物理外键）：**

| 主表 | 从表 | 引用字段 | 校验时机 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `m17_dept_config` | 四张归集表 | `dept_id` | 归集时校验配置存在 |
| `m17_dept_profit` | `m17_dept_profit_detail` | `profit_id` | 生成明细时 |
| `m17_dept_profit` | `m17_dept_compare` | `period + dept_id` | 对比时校验损益存在 |
| M17-01 `m17_account_period` | 全部表 | `period` | 写入前校验期间状态 |
| M01-01 `mdm_dept` | `m17_dept_config` | `dept_id` | 配置时校验科室存在 |

每日 02:00 对账任务检查孤儿记录（从表 `dept_id` 在配置表不存在），输出告警清单，不自动删。

### 8.7 并发控制

| 场景 | 并发风险 | 控制手段 | 失败表现 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **期间内归集并发** | 两次归集同时跑，产生重复记录 | **Redis 期间级锁 `m17:lock:dept:period:revenue:{period}`** + 幂等键唯一索引 | 后到者等待或拒绝 |
| **期间内分摊并发** | 同池被分摊两次 | 期间锁 + `uk_m17_dept_allocation_key` | `"1704014"` |
| **期间内损益计算并发** | 两次计算互相覆盖 | 期间锁 + `uk_m17_dept_profit_key` | `"1704030"` |
| 损益确认并发 | 两人同时确认 | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` 锁期间内全部损益行 | 后到者等待 |
| 配置编辑并发 | 两人改同一配置 | 乐观锁 `version` | `40900` |
| 转移记录并发 | 同参数重复录入 | `uk_m17_dept_transfer_key` | `"1704036"` |
| 重复提交 | 双击产生两次任务 | `Idempotency-Key` + Redis | 返回首次结果 |
| 单号生成 | 并发抢号 | Redis `INCR` 原子自增 | — |
| 报表重算 | 并发重算同一报表 | `m17:lock:dept:report:{period}` | 后到者等待 |

**期间级锁的实现要点：**

```python
# 归集/分摊/计算入口统一获取期间锁
token = str(uuid.uuid4())
acquired = await DeptPeriodLock.acquire(period, biz, token)
if not acquired:
    raise BizException("1704023", f"期间 {period} 正在执行{biz}任务，请稍后重试")
try:
    result = await do_business(period)      # 实际的归集/分摊/计算逻辑
finally:
    await DeptPeriodLock.release(period, biz, token)
```

> **为什么必须用期间级锁而非单行锁：** 归集/分摊/计算的操作对象是「一个期间内的全部科室记录」，涉及数百行。单行锁无法表达「整个期间正在被处理」这一语义，两个任务会各自处理一部分行，最终产生重复或遗漏。期间级分布式锁是唯一正确的粒度。

## 9. 权限与安全

### 9.1 功能权限

| 权限编码 | 说明 | 授予角色 |
|---|---|---|
| `m17:dept:view` | 查看科室配置、归集结果、损益、明细、对比 | 成本会计、财务主管、财务总监、科主任、内控 |
| `m17:dept:create` | 执行归集、分摊、损益计算、对比、新增转移 | 成本会计 |
| `m17:dept:edit` | 修改归集明细、转移记录（草稿态） | 成本会计 |
| `m17:dept:audit` | 确认归集批次、确认转移、确认损益、反发布、作废 | 财务主管 |
| `m17:dept:report` | 查看与发布科室损益报表、统计 | 财务主管、财务总监、院领导 |
| `m17:dept:export` | 导出科室损益 Excel | 财务主管、内控 |
| `m17:dept:admin` | 科室核算参数、费用池配置 | 财务总监、系统管理员 |

> **模块通用权限码：** `"179001"`（M17 无此权限）在本子模块复用，不重复定义。

### 9.3 数据权限

| 维度 | 规则 | 实现 |
|---|---|---|
| **科室** | 科主任只能看本科室损益与明细；成本会计/财务主管看全科室 | Service 层按角色注入 `dept_id` 过滤 |
| **科室树** | 科主任看本人科室及其下级科室汇总 | 按 `parent_dept_id` 递归（`WITH RECURSIVE`） |
| **院区** | 多院区时按 `org_id` 隔离（科室归属院区） | 参数 + 数据权限判断 |
| **本人** | 所有写操作的 `operator_id` 记录当前用户 | 审计追溯 |
| **金额可见性** | 科主任可见本科室收入/成本/结余，**不可见其他科室** | 出参裁剪 |
| **成本明细可见性** | 科主任可见科目级成本，**不可见明细项目与单价** | 出参裁剪（明细行不下发） |
| **对比排名** | 科主任可见本科室排名与百分位，**不可见其他科室具体数值** | Service 层过滤 |
| **暂估数据** | 所有角色可见，但报表明示「暂估」 | `data_status` 标记 |

### 9.4 安全措施

| 措施 | 说明 | 实现要点 |
|---|---|---|
| **结余公式 DB 强制** | 任何路径写入的损益必须满足 `surplus = revenue − direct − allocated` | `ck_m17_dept_profit_formula` |
| **金额禁用浮点** | 全部金额 `NUMERIC(18,2)`，比率 `NUMERIC(9,6)` | 校验脚本扫描 FLOAT/DOUBLE/REAL |
| **分摊尾差强制** | 每池唯一尾差吸收项，合计必须等于池额 | `uk_m17_dept_allocation_tail` + 服务层校验 |
| **内部转移双向强制** | `out_revenue = in_cost = transfer_amount` | `ck_m17_dept_transfer_balance` |
| **期间级并发锁** | 同一期间不允许并发归集/分摊/计算 | Redis 分布式锁 + token 校验 |
| **幂等保护** | 归集/分摊/计算/对比均支持 `Idempotency-Key` | Redis `m17:idem:dept:*` TTL 24h |
| **审计留痕** | 24 个动作全留痕，含金额变更前后值 | `m17_dept_log` + `amount_before/after/delta` |
| **日志只增不改** | 日志表插入后禁止 UPDATE/DELETE | 分区表 + 保留 36 个月 |
| **敏感操作二次确认** | 确认损益、发布、作废、重建 | 前端二次确认 + 后端理由字数校验 |
| **职责分离** | 执行（会计）与确认（主管）分离；发布（主管）与配置（总监）分离 | 见 §9.2 |
| **已确认锁定** | `CONFIRMED` 后金额只读，需先作废才能改 | 状态机 + Service 校验 |
| **暂估不可外发** | `PROVISIONAL` 不可发布到 M22 | API-21 校验 |
| **导出留痕** | 导出金额数据必调 M02-06 审计 | `action='EXPORT'` |
| **传输安全** | 全部 HTTPS；内部契约走 mTLS | 网关配置 |
| **限流** | 按期间查询 60 次/分钟/用户 | Redis 计数 |
| **日志脱敏** | 日志不记录完整患者/职工敏感信息 | `DeptLogService` 统一脱敏 |
